## ०१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/15 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ वै᳓ वाचंयमो᳓ [[भवत्या᳓स्तमेतोरादित्या᳓त्स|भवत्या᳓स्तमे᳓तोरादित्या᳓त्स᳓]] [[यदा᳓स्तमेतोरादित्या᳓द्वाचंयमो᳓|यदा᳓स्तमे᳓तोरादित्या᳓द्वाचंयमो᳓]] भ᳓वति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He restrains his speech (remains silent) till the sun sets; why he maintains silence till the sun sets (is because of this)—
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स वै᳘ वाचंयमो᳘ भवत्या᳘स्तमेतोरादित्यात्स यदा᳘स्तमेतोरादित्या᳘द्वाचंयमो भ᳘वति ॥१॥
</details>

## ०२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/25 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

देवा᳓ वै᳓ यज्ञे᳓नेमां᳓ जि᳓तिं [[जित्वा|जित्वा᳓]] ये᳓य᳓मेषां᳓ जि᳓तिस्ते᳓ होचु᳓र्ह᳓न्तेदं᳓ त᳓था कर᳓वामहा इ᳓ति य᳓था न इद᳓मनभ्यारूह्यं᳓ [[मनुष्यैर᳓सदि᳓ति|मनुष्यै᳙र᳓सदि᳓ति]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Having made this conquest, which conquest is theirs, verily by means of the sacrifice, they, the gods, said, “Well! Let us make this in such a manner that this of ours may be inaccessible to men”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

देवा वै᳘ यज्ञे᳘नेमां जि᳘तिं जित्वा᳘ येय᳘मेषां जि᳘तिस्ते᳘ होचुर्ह᳘न्तेदं त᳘था कर᳘वामहा इ᳘ति य᳘था न इद᳘मनभ्यारूह्यं᳘ मनुष्यैर᳘सदि᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/24 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

ते᳓ यज्ञ᳓स्य र᳓सं नि᳓र्धी᳓य य᳓था मधुकृ᳓तो नि᳓र्ध᳓येयुरेवं᳓ विदु᳓ह्य यज्ञं᳓ यू᳓पेन योपयित्वा᳓ तिरो᳓ [[बभूवुस्त᳓द्य᳓देनेना᳓योपयंस्त᳓स्माद्यू᳓पो|बभूवुस्त᳓द्य᳓देनेनायोपयंस्त᳓स्माद्यू᳓पो]] ना᳓म ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Having completely sucked the essence of the sacrifice and having emptied the sacrifice, just as bees would completely suck (honey), and having obliterated it with the sacrificial post. They disappeared. Because they obliterated (concealed) it with this, therefore is this (sacrificial post) known as ‘Yūpa’ (means of obliteration).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ते᳘ यज्ञ᳘स्य र᳘सं निर्धी᳘य य᳘था मधुकृ᳘तो निर्ध᳘येयुरेवं᳘ विदु᳘ह्य यज्ञं यू᳘पेन योपयित्वा᳘ तिरो᳘ बभूवुस्तद्य᳘देनेना᳘योपयꣳस्त᳘स्माद्यू᳘पो ना᳘म ॥२॥
</details>

## ०३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/24 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[त᳓द्वृषी᳓णाम᳓नुश्रुतमास᳓|त᳓द्वृ᳓षीणाम᳓नुश्रुतमास᳓]] तं᳓ य᳓था य᳓थ᳓र्षयो यज्ञं᳓ सम᳓भरंस्त᳓थायं᳓ यज्ञः᳓ सं᳓भृतो य᳓थो वै᳓ त᳓दृ᳓षयो यज्ञं᳓ सम᳓भरन् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

That indeed was heard by the Ṛṣis. In such and such manner as the Ṛṣis collected the sacrifice, that way the sacrifice was gathered (prepared).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तद्वृषी᳘णाम᳘नुश्रुतमास तं᳘ य᳘था यथ᳘र्षयो यज्ञ᳘ꣳ᳘ सम᳘भरꣳस्त᳘थायं᳘ यज्ञः सं᳘भृतो य᳘थो वै तदृ᳘षयो यज्ञ᳘ꣳ᳘ सम᳘भरन्
</details>

## ०४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/11 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ वै᳓ वा᳓गेव᳓ यज्ञ᳓स्तं᳓ संभृ᳓त्यात्म᳓नि कृत्वा᳓ [[सं᳓य᳓त|सं᳓यत]] आस्ते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Speech is verily the sacrifice. Having gathered (prepared) it and having placed it within himself, he remians with control (of speech) till sunset.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स वै वा᳘गेव᳘ यज्ञस्त᳘ꣳ᳘ संभृ᳘त्यात्म᳘नि कृत्वा सं᳘य᳘त आस्ते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/16 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

आ᳓स्तमेतोरादित्या᳓त्सो᳓ऽस्तमित आदित्ये᳓ वा᳓चं वि᳓सृजते स᳓ य᳓द᳓स्तमित [[आदि᳓त्ये|आदित्ये᳓]] वा᳓चं विसृज᳓ते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When the sun has set, he lets out speech. (The reason) why he lets out speech (breaks his silence) when the Sun has set (is this).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

आ आ᳘स्तमेतोरादित्यात्सो᳘ऽस्तमित आदित्ये वा᳘चं वि᳘सृजते स यद᳘स्तमित आदि᳘त्ये वा᳘चं विसृज᳘ते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/23 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

संवत्सरो᳓ वै᳓ यज्ञः᳓ प्रजा᳓पतिर्हि᳓ [[संवत्सरः|संवत्सरः᳓]] प्रजा᳓पतिर्हि᳓ [[य᳓ज्ञोऽहोरात्रे᳓|यज्ञो᳙ऽहोरात्रे᳓]] उ वै᳓ [[संवत्स᳓र|संवत्सर᳓]] एते᳓ हि᳓ परिप्ल᳓वमाने संवत्सरं᳓ कुरुतः᳓ स᳓ वा᳓ इद᳓म᳓हन्यदीक्षिष्ट ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The year is indeed the sacrifice. Prajāpati is verily the year. Prajāpati is certainly the sacrifice. Indeed day and night (together) are the year. For these two, revolving, bring about the year.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

संवत्सरो वै᳘ यज्ञः᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिर्हि᳘ संवत्सरः प्रजा᳘पतिर्हि य᳘ज्ञोऽहोरात्रे᳘ उ वै᳘ संवत्स᳘र एते हि᳘ परिप्ल᳘वमाने संवत्सरं᳘ कुरुतः स वा᳘ इदम᳘हन्यदीक्षिष्ट
</details>

## ०५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/24 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓द्धै᳓के न᳓क्षत्राणि दर्शयित्वा᳓थ वि᳓सर्जयन्त्येत᳓र्ह्यनुष्ट्या᳓स्तमित इ᳓ति व᳓दन्तस्त᳓दु त᳓था न᳓ [[कुर्या᳓त्क्व᳓|कुर्यात्क्व᳓]] ते᳓ स्युर्य᳓दभ्रः᳓ स्या᳓त् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

In this matter, some make him give up (silence) after pointing out the stars saying, “Now (the sun) has straightway set”. One need not do so, for, where would they be if there should be a cloud?
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तद्धै᳘के न᳘क्षत्राणि दर्शयित्वा᳘थ वि᳘सर्जयन्त्येत᳘र्ह्यनुष्ट्या᳘स्तमित इ᳘ति व᳘दन्तस्त᳘दु त᳘था न᳘ कुर्या᳘क्त्व ते᳘ स्युर्य᳘दभ्रः स्यात्
</details>

## ०६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

सो᳙ऽग्नि᳓मी᳓क्षमाणो वि᳓सृजते व्रतं᳓ कृणुत व्रतं᳓ कृणुत व्रतं᳓ कृणुते᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He lets out speech (speaks), looking at the fire, "Prepare the Vrata (food for the Vrata) "! "Prepare the Vrata! "Prepare the Vrata!" For, it is his 'hairs' (oblation, food).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

सोऽग्निमी᳘क्षमाणो वि᳘सृजते व्रतं᳘ कृणुत व्रतं᳘ कृणुत व्रतं᳘ कृणुते᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/24 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

एत᳓द्धा᳓ एत᳓स्य हवि᳓रेष᳓ यज्ञो᳓ य᳓द्व्रतं᳓ य᳓थास्य पुरा᳙ग्निहोत्रं᳓ त᳓देतं᳓ यज्ञं᳓ संभृ᳓त्यैत᳓स्मिन्यज्ञे᳓ प्र᳓तिष्ठापयति यज्ञे᳓न यज्ञं᳓ [[सं᳓तनो᳓ति|सं᳓तनोति]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

What is Vrata (food for the Vrata), this is the sacrifice, as was the Agnihotra to him formerly. Thus having gathered this sacrifice, he establishes it in this sacrifice.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एतद्धा᳘ एत᳘स्य हवि᳘रेष᳘ यज्ञो य᳘द्व्रतं य᳘थास्य पु᳘राग्निहोत्रं त᳘देतं᳘ यज्ञ᳘ꣳ᳘ संभृ᳘त्यैत᳘स्मिन्यज्ञे प्र᳘तिष्ठापयति यज्ञे᳘न यज्ञꣳ सं᳘तनो᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

सो᳙ऽस्यै᳓ष᳓ सं᳓ततो यज्ञो᳓ भवत्या᳓सुत्या᳓याः सं᳓ततं ह्य᳙स्यै᳓त᳓द्व्रतं᳓ भ᳓वति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He continues the sacrifice by means of the sacrifice. Thus does his sacrifice become drawn out (continuously) till the pressing of the soma. Indeed thus his Vrata (food for Vrata) becomes continuous.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

सोऽस्यैष सं᳘ततो यज्ञो᳘ भवत्या᳘सुत्या᳘याः सं᳘तत᳘ꣳ᳘ ह्यस्यैत᳘द्व्रतं भ᳘वति ॥६॥

## ०७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/11 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓दु है᳓के भू᳓र्भु᳓वः स्व᳙रि᳓ति वा [[विसर्ज᳓यन्ति|वि᳓सर्जयन्ति]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

In that matter, some make him break his silence, verily (with the utterance), "Bhuh", (Earth), "Bhuvah" (midregion), "Svah" (Heaven, sky) or with some thing else.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दु है᳘के भूर्भु᳘वः᳘ स्वरि᳘ति वा विसर्ज᳘यन्ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अन्ये᳓न वा न᳓ ह ते᳓ यज्ञं᳓ सं᳓दधति स᳓ वै᳓ त्रि᳓राह त्रिवृ᳓द्धि᳓ यज्ञः᳓ ॥

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

## ०८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थाहाग्नि᳓र्ब्र᳓ह्माग्नि᳓र्यज्ञो᳓ व᳓नस्प᳓तिर्यज्ञि᳓य इ᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he says, "Agni is the Brahman. Agni is the sacrifice. The tree is fit for the sacrifice". Thus he first utters with his speech, verily the truth now. For, Agni is 'Brahman' and Agni is indeed the sacrifice and the trees are verily fit for the sacrifice.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थाहाग्निर्ब्र᳘ह्माग्नि᳘र्यज्ञो व᳘नस्प᳘तिर्यज्ञि᳘य इ᳘ति
</details>

## ०९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/19 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ [[कामं᳓|का᳓मं]] वावद्यते त᳓देत᳓द्वा᳓ एत᳓स्य हवि᳓रेष᳓ यज्ञो᳓ य᳓द्व्रतं᳓ य᳓थास्य [[पुराग्निहोत्रं|पुरा᳓ग्निहोत्रं᳓]] त᳓स्मादेत᳓च्छ्रपयन्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Thereafter he speaks profusely as he likes. Now this indeed (viz. the Vrata) (food for Vrata) is his 'hair' (oblation), and this is the sacrifice, just as Agnihotra was formerly to him. Therefore they cook it, for the cooked (food) is for the gods. Then he himself consumes it (as food for Vrata). They do not offer it in the fire. (The reason) Why he himself eats it (for Vrata) and they do not offer it into the fire (is this)—
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ कामं᳘ वावद्यते त᳘देतद्वा᳘ एत᳘स्य हवि᳘रेष᳘ यज्ञो य᳘द्व्रतं य᳘थास्य पुराग्निहोत्रं त᳘स्मादेत᳘च्छ्रपयन्ति
</details>

## १०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/25 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

देवा᳓ वै᳓ यज्ञे᳓नेमां᳓ जि᳓तिं [[जित्वा|जित्वा᳓]] ये᳓य᳓मेषां᳓ [[जितिस्ते᳓|जि᳓तिस्ते᳓]] होचु᳓र्ह᳓न्तेदं᳓ त᳓था कर᳓वामहा इ᳓ति य᳓था न इद᳓मनभ्यारुह्यं᳓ [[मनुष्यैर᳓सदि᳓ति|मनुष्यै᳙र᳓सदि᳓ति]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The gods, having indeed won by means of the sacrifice this conquest, which is their conquest, said, "Well! Let us make this in such a manner that this (sacrifice) of ours would not be accessible to men".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

देवा वै᳘ यज्ञे᳘नेमां जि᳘तिं जित्वा᳘ येय᳘मेषां जितिस्ते᳘ होचुर्ह᳘न्तेदं त᳘था कर᳘वामहा इ᳘ति य᳘था न इद᳘मनभ्यारुह्यं᳘ मनुष्यैर᳘सदि᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (4/24 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

ते᳓ यज्ञ᳓स्य [[रसं|र᳓सं]] [[नि᳓र्धीय|नि᳓र्धी᳓य]] य᳓था मधुकृ᳓तो नि᳓र्ध᳓येयुरेवं᳓ विदु᳓ह्य यज्ञं᳓ यू᳓पेन [[योपयित्वा|योपयित्वा᳓]] तिरो᳓ [[बभूवुस्त᳓द्य᳓देनेना᳓योपयंस्त᳓स्माद्यू᳓पो|बभूवुस्त᳓द्य᳓देनेनायोपयंस्त᳓स्माद्यू᳓पो]] ना᳓म ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Having completely sucked the essence of the sacrifice, as bees would suck completely, and having thus emptied the sacrifice and then having obliterated it with the sacrificial post, they disappeared. Since they obliterated it by means of this, hence this (sacrificial post) is known as 'Yupa'.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ते᳘ यज्ञ᳘स्य रसं निर्धीय य᳘था मधुकृ᳘तो निर्ध᳘येयुरेवं᳘ विदु᳘ह्य यज्ञं यू᳘पेन योपयित्वा तिरो᳘ बभूवुस्तद्य᳘देनेना᳘योपयꣳस्त᳘स्माद्यू᳘पो ना᳘म ॥१०॥
</details>

## ११

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/26 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[त᳓दृषीणाम᳓नुश्रुतमास|त᳓दृ᳓षीणाम᳓नुश्रुतमास]] तं᳓ य᳓था य᳓थ᳓र्षयो यज्ञं᳓ सम᳓भरंस्त᳓थायं᳓ यज्ञः᳓ सं᳓भृतः स᳓ वा᳓ एष᳓ एव᳓ यज्ञो᳓ यो᳓ दी᳓क्षत एष᳓ ह्ये᳙नं [[त᳓नुते|तनुते᳓]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

But that was heard by the Ṛṣis. In just what manner and how the Ṛṣis collected that sacrifice, in that way has this sacrifice been prepared. This person who undertakes the vow is himself indeed the sacrifice. For it is he who performs it; he indeed produces it.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तद्बृषीणाम᳘नुश्रुतमास तं य᳘था यथ᳘र्षयो यज्ञ᳘ꣳ᳘ सम᳘भरꣳस्त᳘थायं᳘ यज्ञः सं᳘भृतः स वा᳘ एष᳘ एव᳘ यज्ञो यो दी᳘क्षत एष᳘ ह्येनं त᳘नुते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

एष᳓ ह्ये᳙नं जन᳓यते स᳓ य᳓देव᳓ त᳓त्र यज्ञ᳓स्य देवै᳓र्नि᳓र्धीतं य᳓द्वि᳓दुग्धं त᳓देवै᳙ते᳓न हवि᳓षैते᳓न र᳓सेना᳓प्याययति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

What verily, then, of the sacrifice had been completely sucked by the gods, what had been emptied, that very thing does he replenish (fill up) by means of this 'havis' (oblation, food), by means of this essence.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एष᳘ ह्येनं जन᳘यते स य᳘देव त᳘त्र यज्ञ᳘स्य देवैर्नि᳘र्धीतं यद्वि᳘दुग्धं त᳘दे᳘वैते᳘न हवि᳘षैते᳘न र᳘सेना᳘प्याययति
</details>

न᳓ हा᳓प्याययेद्य᳓दग्नौ᳓ जुहुया᳓त्त᳓स्मादेष᳓ एव᳓ व्रत᳓यति ना᳙ग्नौ᳓ जुह्वति ॥

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

## १२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/33 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓दु जु᳓ह्वदेव᳓ मन्येतेमे᳓ वै᳓ प्राणा᳓ म᳓नोजाता मनोयु᳓जो द᳓क्षक्रतवोऽग्नि᳓रेव᳓ [[वाङ्मित्रा᳓व᳓रुणौ|वा᳓ङ्मित्रा᳓व᳓रुणौ]] प्राणोदानौ᳓ सू᳓र्यश्च᳓क्षुर्वि᳓श्वे देवाः᳓ श्रो᳓त्रं त᳓दस्यै᳓ता᳓सु देव᳓तासु य᳓था हुत᳓मेवं᳓ भवति त᳓स्माज्जु᳓ह्वदेव᳓ मन्येत ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then, he should think (in his mind) verily that he is offering, "These vital airs (energies/faculties) are born of the mind, united with the mind and of dexterous functions. Agni verily is speech, Mitra and Varuṇa are the (two breaths) prāṇa and the Sun is the eye and the 'Viśvadevas' are the ear". Thus, it becomes such that it is properly offered into these deities. Therefore he should indeed think that he is making the offering.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दु जु᳘ह्वदेव᳘ मन्येतेमे वै᳘ प्राणा म᳘नोजाता मनोयु᳘जो द᳘क्षक्रतवोऽग्नि᳘रेव वाङ्मित्राव᳘रुणौ प्राणोदानौ सू᳘र्यश्च᳘क्षुर्वि᳘श्वे देवाः श्रो᳘त्रं त᳘दस्यैता᳘सु देव᳘तासु य᳘था हुत᳘मेवं᳘ भवति त᳘स्माज्जु᳘ह्वदेव᳘ मन्येत ॥१२॥
</details>

## १३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/36 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓द्धै᳓के प्रथमे᳓ व्रत᳓ उभौ᳓ व्रीहियवा᳓वा᳓वपन्त्युभा᳓भ्यां चैता᳓भ्यां र᳓साभ्यां य᳓द्यज्ञ᳓स्य नि᳓र्धीतं य᳓द्वि᳓दुग्धं त᳓दा᳓प्याययिष्याम इ᳓त्य᳓थो उत᳓ [[गौ᳓रवभिनत्ती᳓ति|गौ᳓र᳓वभिनत्ती᳓ति]] स᳓ [[यतरस्यातः|यतर᳓स्या᳓तः]] काम᳓येते᳓ति त᳓स्य व्रतं᳓ श्रपयेयुः ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

In that matter, some mix both rice and barley in the first Vrata (food for the Vrata), (thinking), "What, of the sacrifice, has been sucked completely and what has been emptied, that we shall fill up (replenish) by means of these two essences (things with essence)". "And if more over, the cow fails (to yield milk), of these whichever he may like they should cook (that as) his food for the vow; they should make porridge for him".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तद्धै᳘के प्रथमे᳘ व्रत᳘ उभौ᳘ व्रीहियवावा᳘वपन्त्युभा᳘भ्यां चैता᳘भ्याꣳ र᳘साभ्यां य᳘द्यज्ञ᳘स्य नि᳘र्धीतं यद्वि᳘दुग्धं तदा᳘प्याययिष्याम इत्य᳘थो उत गौरवभिनत्ती᳘ति स᳘ यतरस्यातः काम᳘येते᳘ति त᳘स्य व्रत᳘ꣳ᳘ श्रपयेयुः
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓स्य यवागूं᳓ कुर्युरि᳓ति त᳓दस्य᳓ य᳓था हवि᳓रा᳓रब्धमि᳓त्येव᳓मस्य᳓ त᳓द्भवती᳓ति त᳓दु त᳓था न᳓ कुर्याद्धवि᳓र्वा᳓ एत᳓द्य᳓द्व्रीहियवौ᳓ हविः᳓ प᳓यो य᳓द्वा᳓ एतौ᳓ हवि᳓स्ते᳓नैवा᳙स्यै᳓ता᳓वा᳓रब्धौ त᳓स्माद᳓प्यन्यतर᳓मेवा᳓वपेद्य᳓द्यु काम᳓येता᳓पि प᳓योव्रत एव᳓ स्यात् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

"Then just as (the material for) his 'havis' (food for consecration) is taken thus does it (the sacrifice) then become for him". But he need not do it so. These two things viz., rice and barley are indeed sacrificial food. Milk is sacrificial food. If therefore these two (rice and barley) have been taken as (material for) 'havis' for him, even with that they may mix (milk); only any one of the two. Should he (so desire) he may take merely milk as food (for the consecration).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘स्य यवागूं᳘ कुर्युरि᳘ति त᳘दस्य य᳘था हविरा᳘रब्धमि᳘त्येव᳘मस्य त᳘द्भवती᳘ति त᳘दु त᳘था न᳘ कुर्याद्धविर्वा᳘ एतद्य᳘द्वौहियवौ᳘ हविः प᳘यो यद्वा᳘ एतौ᳘ हविस्ते᳘नै᳘वास्यैतावा᳘रब्धौ त᳘स्माद᳘प्यन्यतर᳘मेवा᳘वपेद्य᳘द्यु काम᳘येता᳘पि प᳘योव्रत एव᳘ स्यात् ॥१३॥
</details>

## १४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓द्धै᳓के प्रथमे᳓ व्रते᳓ सर्वौषधं᳓ सर्वसुरभ्या᳓वपन्त्युत᳓ दीक्षित᳓मा᳓र्तिर्विन्दती᳓ति त᳓तो ये᳓नागदं᳓ भविष्य᳓न्तं म᳓न्यन्ते ते᳓न भिषज्यन्ती᳓ति स᳓ य᳓था हविषा᳓रब्धेन भिषज्ये᳓दि᳓त्येव᳓मेत᳓दि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Now, some mix (with the 'Vrata'milk) all vegetable and fragrant (season- ing substances) (thinking), "Perhaps affliction befalls (may befall) this person who has undertaken the vow. Then, with that, by which they think he will become free from disease, they treat (cure) him. This is just like that he may (cure) treat himself with the 'havis' (the Vrata food) that has been taken (by him)".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तद्धै᳘के प्रथमे᳘ व्रते᳘ सर्वौषध᳘ꣳ᳘ सर्वसुरभ्या᳘वपन्त्युत᳘ दीक्षितमा᳘र्तिर्विन्दती᳘ति त᳘तो ये᳘नागदं᳘ भविष्य᳘न्तं म᳘न्यन्ते ते᳘न भिषज्यन्ती᳘ति स य᳘था हविषा᳘रब्धेन भिषज्येदि᳘त्येव᳘मेतदि᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/21 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓दु त᳓था न᳓ [[कुर्यान्मानु᳓षं᳓|कुर्यान्मानुषं᳓]] ह कुर्याद्य᳓त्त᳓था कुर्या᳓द्व्यृ᳙द्धं वै᳓ त᳓द्यज्ञ᳓स्य य᳓न्मानुषं᳓ त᳓स्मात्त᳓था न᳓ कुर्यात् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He should not, however, do so. For, if he should do so, he would indeed be doing a human act. What is human—that indeed is the decline (ruin) of the sacrifice. Therefore he should not do so.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दु त᳘था न᳘ कुर्यान्मानु᳘ष᳘ꣳ᳘ ह कुर्याद्यत्त᳘था कुर्या᳘द्व्यृद्धं वै त᳘द्यज्ञ᳘स्य य᳘न्मानुषं त᳘स्मात्त᳘था न᳘ कुर्यात्
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/28 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓माप्तिर्ह्ये᳙व᳓ पु᳓ण्या सो᳙ऽतिनी᳓येव तं᳓ कालं᳓ प्र᳓यच्छन्ति य᳓त्र मनुष्या᳙ [[अ᳓श्नन्त्यपरमिवा᳓नुरात्रेरपराह्णे᳓|अश्न᳓न्त्यपरमिवा᳓नुरा᳓त्रेरपराह्णे᳓]] प्रातर्दुग्ध᳓म᳓मानुषेणैवै᳙नं त᳓द्दे᳓वेनैवो᳓पचरति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Therefore, they give (that food) to him, passing over the (usual) time when men eat i.e. in the latter part of the night (the milk of the afternoon), and (give him) the morning milk in the afternoon. By that he (Adhvaryu) treats this (consecrated) person in a non-human, in a verily divine, way.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स᳘माप्ति᳘र्ह्येव पु᳘ण्या᳘ सोऽतिनी᳘येव तं᳘ कालं प्र᳘यच्छन्ति य᳘त्र मनु᳘ष्या अ᳘श्नन्त्यपरमिवा᳘नुरात्रेरपराह्णे᳘ प्रातर्दुग्धम᳘मानुषेणै᳘वैनं तद्दे᳘वेनैवो᳘पचरति ॥१४॥
</details>

## १५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ य᳓त्रास्मै व्रतं᳓ प्रदास्य᳓न्भ᳓वति त᳓द᳓वनेजयति ।
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When, he is about to hand over the food of 'Vrata' to this (sacrificer) he makes him wash himself.
</details>

स य᳘त्रास्मै व्रतं᳘ प्रदास्यन्भ᳘वति तद᳘वनेजयति

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/15 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

सो᳓ऽवनेनिक्ते दै᳓वीं धि᳓यं मनामहे [[सुमृलीका᳓मभिष्टये|सुमृलीका᳓मभि᳓ष्टये]] ।  
वर्चोदां᳓ विश्व᳓धायसं सुतीर्था᳓ नो असद्व᳓श इ᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He (the sacrificer) washes himself, (chanting) "We meditate on the divine 'Intelligence' which is very merciful (which produces happiness), for the sake of help—the 'Intelligence' which bears (bestows) brilliance and which sustains all (the Universe). May it which is (provider of) a good food (to cross over) be in our power".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

सो᳘ऽवनेनिक्ते दै᳘वीं धि᳘यं मनामहे सुमृलीका᳘मभिष्टये । वर्चोदां᳘ विश्व᳘धायसꣳ सुतीर्था᳘ नो असद्व᳘श इ᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/18 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

मानुषा᳓य वा᳓ इदं᳓ पुरा᳓वनेनिक्ते᳓ऽथैत᳓द्दै᳓व्यै धिये᳓ य᳓द्दीक्षित᳓स्त᳓स्मादाह᳓ दै᳓वीं धि᳓यं मनामह [[इति|इ᳓ति]] ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Thus, formerly he washed himself for a human (food). And now it is for the sake of the divine 'Intelligence' that he who has undertaken the vow (washes himself). Therefore he says, "We think of (meditate upon) the divine Intelligence".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

मानुषा᳘य वा᳘ इदं᳘ पुरा᳘वनेनिक्ते᳘ऽथैतद्दै᳘व्यै धिये य᳘द्दीक्षितस्त᳘स्मादाह दै᳘वीं धि᳘यं मनामह इति ॥१५॥
</details>

## १६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थास्मै व्रतं᳓ प्र᳓यच्छन्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then, they give him the Vrata food. Then he ceremoniously sips water, (chanting) "Those gods who are born of the mind, who unite with (who employ) the mind, who are of dexterous action may they protect us, may they guard us!" To them—Svāhā!"
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थास्मै व्रतं प्र᳘यच्छन्ति
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः)</summary>

- अ᳓थ (*TS*-161, *RV*-117, *TB*-146, *TK*-34, *AV*-68, *KS*-37, *MS*-482)
- अस्मै (अन्वादेशे अनुदात्तम्, पा॰ २.४.३२, *TS*-286, *RV*-132, *TB*-49, *TK*-11, *AV*-65, *KS*-29, *MS*-115)
- व्रत᳓म् (*TS*-35/49, *RV*-33/100, *TB*-15/23, *TK*-1, *AV*-12/29, *SB-M*-67/89, *VS-M*-4/13, *VS-K*-7/16, *KS*-7/16, *MS*-44/72)
- प्र᳓ (*RV*-1193, *TS*-780, *AV*-568, *VS-M*-203, *TB*-401, *TK*-34, *KS*-205, *MS*-829)
- यच्छन्ति (तिङन्तम् अनुदात्तम्, पा॰ ८.१.२८, *TS*-15, *RV*-3, *TB*-3, *AV*-1, *KS*-1)
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓दा᳓चामति ये᳓ देवा᳓ म᳓नोजाता मनोयु᳓जो द᳓क्षक्रतवः ।  
ते᳓ नोऽवन्तु ते᳓ नः पान्तु ते᳓भ्यः स्वा᳓हे᳓तीमे᳓ वै᳓ प्राणा᳓ म᳓नोजाता मनोयु᳓जो द᳓क्षक्रतवस्त᳓दस्यै᳓षु᳓ प्राणे᳓षु य᳓था हुतं᳓ स्वा᳓हाकृतं स्या᳓देवं᳓ भवति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

These vital airs (energies) are indeed the mind-born ones, which yoke the mind and which are of dexterous action. Therefore, thus it becomes such that it is offered, with the utterance of 'Svāhā!' into these vital airs (energies) of this person (sacrificer).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

## १७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

व्रतयित्वा᳙प᳓ उपस्पृ᳓श्योद᳓रमभि᳓मृशते श्वात्राः᳓ पीता᳓ भवत यूय᳓मापो अस्मा᳓कमन्त᳓रुद᳓रे सुशे᳓वाः ।  
ता᳓ अस्म᳓भ्यमयक्ष्मा᳓ अनमीवा᳓ अ᳓नागसः स्व᳓दन्तु देवी᳓रमृ᳓ता ऋतावृ᳓ध इ᳓ति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Having consumed the 'Vrata' food, and (then) having touched water, he strokes his belly (chanting) "Oh extensive waters! Having been drunk, you please become friendly within our belly! May they (the waters) be tasty to us, without disease (without consumption), without illness, and without sin—they, the divine, the immortal and the holy (fosterers of Truth)".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

व्रतयि᳘त्वाप᳘ उपस्पृ᳘श्योद᳘रमभि᳘मृशते श्वात्राः᳘ पीता᳘ भवत यूय᳘मापो अस्मा᳘कमन्त᳘रुद᳘रे सुशे᳘वाः । ता᳘ अस्म᳘भ्यमयक्ष्मा᳘ अनमीवा अ᳘नागसः स्व᳘दन्तु देवी᳘रमृ᳘ता ऋतावृ᳘ध इ᳘ति ॥१७॥
</details>

यो᳓ दी᳓क्षते स᳓ देव᳓तानामेवै᳓को भवत्य᳓नुत्सिक्तमु वै᳓ देवा᳓नां [[हविस्तत्त᳓न्मिथ्या᳓करोति|हवि᳓स्तत्त᳓न्मिथ्या᳓करोति]] व्रतं᳓ प्र᳓मिनाति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indeed he who undertakes the vow approaches the gods. He verily becomes one of the gods themselves. The food of the gods (the 'havis') is not sprinkled (not added to).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

यो दी᳘क्षते स᳘ देव᳘तानामेवै᳘को भवत्य᳘नुत्सिक्तमु वै᳘ देवा᳘नाꣳ हविस्तत्त᳘न्मिथ्या᳘करोति व्रतं प्र᳘मिनाति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/33 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

य᳓दुपोत्सिञ्च᳓ति त᳓थास्य᳓ त᳓न्न᳓ मिथ्या᳓कृतं भ᳓वति त᳓था व्रतं᳓ न᳓ [[प्र᳓मिनात्ये᳙षो᳙ऽत्र|प्र᳓मिनात्येषो᳓ऽत्र]] [[प्रा᳓यश्चित्तिस्त᳓दभिमृश्यमेव᳓|प्रा᳓यश्चित्तिस्त᳓दभिमृ᳓श्यमेव᳓]] को᳓ हि᳓ त᳓द्वे᳓द य᳓दि व्रतप्रद᳓ उ᳓प वोत्सिञ्च᳓ति य᳓था वा ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When he sprinkles, he makes it wrongly and violates the vow. Thereby (by chanting) this (vow) of his does not become 'wrongly done' and thereby he does not violate the vow. This (chant) is indeed the atonement. Therefore it (the belly) should be certainly stroked. Who, indeed, knows whether the giver of the Vrata food sprinkles (increases) and how (he sprinkles)?"
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘दुपोत्सिञ्च᳘ति त᳘थास्य तन्न᳘ मिथ्या᳘कृतं भ᳘वति त᳘था व्रतं न प्र᳘मिनात्ये᳘षो᳘ऽत्र प्रा᳘यश्चित्तिस्त᳘दभिमृश्य मेव को हि तद्वे᳘द य᳘दि व्रतप्रद उ᳘प वोत्सिञ्च᳘ति य᳘था वा ॥१८॥
</details>

## १९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/19 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓त्र [[प्र᳓स्रावयिष्य᳓माणो|प्रस्रावयिष्य᳓माणो]] भ᳓वति त᳓त्कृष्णविषाण᳓यो᳓पहन्ति पांसू᳓न्वा परिपदं᳓ वा लोकं᳓ वेयं᳓ ते यज्ञि᳓या तनू᳓रि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

And, then, where he is going to urinate, he strikes with the black antelope horn either the dust or the place around or the ground (chanting) "This is your body, fit for (the performance of) sacrifice".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘त्र प्र᳘स्रावयिष्य᳘माणो भ᳘वति त᳘त्कृष्णविषाणयो᳘पहन्ति पाꣳसू᳘न्वा परिपदं᳘ वा लोकं᳘ वेयं᳘ ते यज्ञि᳘या तनूरि᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/44 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓र्वा वा᳓ इयं᳓ पृथिवी᳓ देवी᳓ [[देवयजनं᳓|देवय᳓जनं]] से᳙यं᳓ दीक्षिते᳓न ना᳙भिमि᳓ह्य त᳓स्या एत᳓द्य᳓देव᳓ यज्ञि᳓यं [[त᳓दुद्गृह्णत्य᳓थास्या|त᳓दु᳓द्गृह्णत्य᳓थास्या]] अयज्ञिय᳓मेव᳓ श᳓रीरमभि᳓मेहत्यपो᳓ मुञ्चामि न᳓ प्रजा᳓मि᳓ति प्र᳓स्रावयते᳓ऽतो वै᳓ रे᳓त एति त᳓दात्म᳓न्येव᳓ प्रजां᳓ कुरुतेऽप᳓ एव᳓ मुञ्चति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This whole earth is certainly divine and it is the place of worship of the gods. Such a one, this (earth) should not be urinated upon by the person who has taken up the vow. What is sacrifice-worthy of that very earth—that he takes away thus and he urinates upon only what body of this (earth) is unfit for sacrifice. "I release the waters (of urine) and not progeny (the seed)". So (chanting) he urinates. Indeed from this (passage of urine) semen (too) issues. Thus (by chanting) he retains the progeny (seed) within himself and lets out only urine.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स᳘र्वा वा᳘ इयं᳘ पृथिवी᳘ देवी᳘ देवयजन᳘ꣳ᳘ सेयं᳘ दीक्षिते᳘न᳘ नाभिमि᳘ह्य त᳘स्या एतद्य᳘देव᳘ यज्ञि᳘यं त᳘दुद्गृह्णत्य᳘थास्या अयज्ञिय᳘मेव श᳘रीरमभि᳘मेहत्यपो᳘ मुञ्चामि न᳘ प्रजामि᳘ति प्र᳘स्रावयते᳘ऽतो वै रे᳘त एति त᳘दात्म᳘न्येव᳘ प्रजां᳘ कुरुतेऽप᳘ एव᳘ मुञ्चति
</details>

- स᳓र्वाः (*TS*-51/287, *RV*-12/68, *TB*-34/159, *TK*-58/114, *AV*-67/444, *SB-M*-143/2265, *VS-M*-13/55, *VS-K*-8/41, *KS*-11/43, *MS*-101/419)

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓स्मादाहापो᳓ मुञ्चामि न᳓ प्रजा᳓मि᳓त्यंहोमु᳓चः स्वा᳓हाकृताः पृथिवी᳓मा᳓विशते᳓त्यं᳓हस इव वा᳓ एता᳓ मुञ्चन्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Therefore does he say, "I release the waters, not progeny".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/7 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

य᳓दिह [[किञ्चिदुपवेष्टितं|कि᳓ञ्चिदु᳓पवेष्टितं]] वा [[भवति|भ᳓वति]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

(He chants) "Deliverers from trouble, (sanctified) with the utterance of 'Svāhā', (you Oh waters!) enter the earth!" These (waters of urine) indeed release from trouble what is pressed here (in his body). Therefore he says, "Release from trouble".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘दिह किञ्चिदुपवेष्टितं वा भवति
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः - 3/7 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

य᳓द्वा त᳓स्मादाहांहोमु᳓च इ᳓ति स्वा᳓हाकृताः पृथिवी᳓मा᳓विशते᳓त्या᳓हुतिमेवै᳙नामेत᳓त्कृत्वा᳓ शमयित्वा᳓ पृथिवीं᳓ प्रा᳓पयति त᳓स्मादाह पृथिवी᳓मा᳓विशते᳓ति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

(By saying) "Having been sanctified by (the utterance of) 'Svāhā', enter the earth he makes them reach the earth after having thus made them verily into an offering and after having appeased them. Hence says he, "Enter the earth".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘द्वा त᳘स्मादाहꣳहोमु᳘च इ᳘ति स्वा᳘हाकृताः पृथिवीमा᳘विशतेत्या᳘हुतिमे᳘वैनामेत᳘त्कृत्वा᳘ शमयित्वा᳘ पृथिवीं प्रा᳘पयति त᳘स्मादाह पृथिवीमा᳘विशते᳘ति ॥१९॥
</details>

## २०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/7 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[प्र᳓स्रा᳓व्य|प्रस्रा᳓व्य]] [[पुन᳓र्न्य᳙स्यति|पु᳓नर्न्य᳙स्यति]] पृथिव्या᳓ सं᳓भवे᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Having urinated, he replaces (the earth taken away formerly) (chanting) "Unite with the earth".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

प्र᳘स्रा᳘व्य पुन᳘र्न्यस्यति पृथिव्या सं᳘भवे᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः - 2/7 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/17 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[त᳓द्य᳓देवा᳙स्या|त᳓द्य᳓देवा᳙स्या᳓]] यज्ञि᳓यं त᳓देत᳓दुद्गृ᳓ह्यायज्ञियं᳓ श᳓रीरमभिमि᳓ह्य त᳓दस्या᳓मेव᳓ पु᳓नर्यज्ञि᳓यं दधाति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Thus, having taken up (away) what is sacrifice-worthy of this (earth) and having urinated upon that part (body) unfit-for sacrifice, he puts back again on it the sacrifice-worthy part.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तद्य᳘दे᳘वास्या यज्ञि᳘यं त᳘देत᳘दुद्गृ᳘ह्यायज्ञियꣳ श᳘रीरमभिमि᳘ह्य त᳘दस्या᳘मेव पु᳘नर्यज्ञि᳘यं दधाति
</details>

## २१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/8 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ [[यत्र|य᳓त्र]] स्वप्स्य᳓न्भ᳓वति त᳓दग्न᳓ये परिदा᳓य स्वपिति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Now, where he is going to sleep, having, given it (that place) to Agni (for protection)?
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ यत्र स्वप्स्यन्भ᳘वति त᳘दग्न᳘ये परिदा᳘य स्वपिति
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः - 1/8 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

- भ᳓वति (यद्वृत्तान्नित्यम् पा॰ ८.१.६६, *Whitney*, *Lanman*, *TS*-135, *TB*-55, *RV*-10, *AV*-10, *KS*-28, *MS*-122)

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/37 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

देवा᳓न्वा᳓ एष᳓ उपा᳓वर्तते यो᳓ दी᳓क्षते [[स|स᳓]] देव᳓तानामेवै᳓को भवति न᳓ वै᳓ देवाः᳓ स्वपन्त्य᳓नवरुद्ध उ वा᳓ एत᳓स्या᳓स्वप्नोऽग्नि᳓रु वै᳓ देवा᳓नां व्रत᳓पतिस्त᳓द्य᳓ एव᳓ देवा᳓नां व्रत᳓पतिस्त᳓स्मा एवै᳙त᳓त्परिदा᳓य स्वपिति᳓ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indeed he who undertakes the vow approaches the gods. He verily becomes one of the gods themselves. The gods surely do not sleep. Sleeplessness is for this (sacrifice) not apportioned indeed. Agni is verily the lord of vows to the gods. Thus, having handed over (the place) to him who himself is the lord of vows to the gods, he sleeps.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

देवान्वा᳘ एष᳘ उपा᳘वर्तते यो दी᳘क्षते स देव᳘तानामेवै᳘को भवति न वै᳘ देवाः᳘ स्वपन्त्य᳘नवरुद्ध उ वा᳘ एतस्या᳘स्वप्नोऽग्नि᳘रु वै᳘ देवा᳘नां व्रत᳘पतिस्तद्य᳘ एव᳘ देवा᳘नां व्रत᳘पतिस्त᳘स्मा ए᳘वैत᳘त्परिदा᳘य स्वपिति ॥२१॥
</details>

## २२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/45 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ आहा᳓ग्ने त्वं᳓ सु᳓जागृहि वयं᳓ [[सु|सु᳓]] मन्दिषीमही᳓ति त्वं᳓ जागृहि वयं᳓ स्वप्स्याम इ᳓त्येवै᳙त᳓दाह र᳓क्ष णो᳓ अ᳓प्रयुच्छन्नि᳓ति गोपाय᳓ नो᳓ऽप्रमत्त इ᳓त्येवै᳙त᳓दाह प्रबु᳓धे नः पु᳓नस्कृधी᳓ति य᳓था प्रबु᳓ध्यामहै त᳓था नः पु᳓नः कुर्वि᳓त्येवै᳙त᳓दाह ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He says, "Oh Agni! You please wake up (be alert), well. May we well sleep (rejoice)!" He verily says (means) this, "You be awake, we shall sleep". (He chants), "Protect us, being attentive (not inattentive)". He verily says (means) this only, "Not being inattentive, guard us". (He says), "Make us wake up again".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स᳘ आहा᳘ग्ने त्वꣳ सु᳘जागृहि वयꣳ सु मन्दिषीमही᳘ति त्वं᳘ जागृहि वय᳘ꣳ᳘ स्वप्स्याम इ᳘त्ये᳘वैत᳘दाह र᳘क्ष णो अ᳘प्रयुच्छन्नि᳘ति गोपाय नो᳘ऽप्रमत्त इ᳘त्ये᳘वैत᳘दाह प्रबु᳘धे नः पु᳘नस्कृधी᳘ति य᳘था प्रबु᳘ध्यामहै त᳘था नः पु᳘नः कुर्वि᳘त्ये᳘वैत᳘दाह
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

प्रबु᳓ध्य य᳓तः पु᳓नर्न᳓ स्वप्स्य᳓न्भ᳓वति ॥

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

## २३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓ज्जपति पु᳓नर्म᳓नः पु᳓नरा᳓युर्म आ᳓गात्पु᳓नश्च᳓क्षुः पु᳓नः श्रो᳓त्रं म आ᳓गात्पु᳓नः प्राणः᳓ पु᳓नरात्मा᳓ म आ᳓गादि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he mutters meditatively, "My mind has come back to me; my life again (has come back to me). My eye has come back to me, my ear again (has come back to me). My breath has come back to me, my soul again (has come back to me)".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘ज्जपति पु᳘नर्म᳘नः पु᳘नरा᳘युर्म आ᳘गात्पु᳘नश्च᳘क्षुः पु᳘नः श्रो᳘त्रं म आ᳘गात्पु᳘नः प्राणः पु᳘नरात्मा᳘ म आ᳘गादि᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓र्वा वा᳓ अस्मा᳓देता᳓ देव᳓ता अ᳓पक्रान्ताः स्व᳓पतो भवन्ति प्राण᳓ एवा᳙स्मा᳓द᳓नपक्रान्तो भवति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indeed all these deities (faculties) go away from this person who sleeps. Only his vital energy does not depart from him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स᳘र्वा वा᳘ अस्मा᳘देता᳘ देव᳘ता अ᳘पक्रान्ताः स्व᳘पतो भवन्ति प्राण᳘ ए᳘वास्माद᳘नपक्रान्तो भवति
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

## २४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/29 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓द्यव्रत्यां᳓ का᳓ञ्चिद्वा᳓चमपव्याह᳓रेत्क्रुद्धो᳓ वा प्र᳓मत्तो वा त᳓दु [[जपेत्त्वमग्ने|जपेत्त्व᳓मग्ने]] व्रतपा᳓ असि देव᳓ आ᳓ म᳓र्त्येष्वा᳓ ।  
त्वं᳓ यज्ञे᳓ष्वी᳓ड्य इ᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

And, if being angry or inadvertent, he should utter some irrelevant expression unworthy of the vow, then he should mutter, "Oh Agni! You are the protector of the vow, Oh god! among men. You are fit to be praised in sacrifices".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘द्यव्रत्यां का᳘ञ्चिद्वा᳘चमपव्याह᳘रेत्क्रुद्धो᳘ वा प्र᳘मत्तो वा त᳘दु जपेत्त्वमग्ने व्रतपा᳘ असि देव आ म᳘र्त्येष्वा । त्वं᳘ यज्ञेष्वी᳘ड्य इ᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/24 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अग्नि᳓र्वै᳓ देवा᳓नां व्रत᳓पतिस्त᳓मेवै᳙त᳓दु᳓पधावति त᳓थास्य᳓ [[त᳓न्नाव्रत्यं᳓|त᳓न्ना᳓व्रत्यं᳓]] कृतं᳓ भ᳓वति त᳓था व्रतं᳓ न᳓ [[प्र᳓मिनात्ये᳓षोऽत्र|प्र᳓मिनात्येषो᳓ऽत्र]] प्रा᳓यश्चित्तिः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indeed, Agni is the Lord of vows to gods. Thus he (the sacrificer) approaches (rushes to) him. Thereby his action does not become inconsistent with the vow. Thus he does not violate the vow. This indeed is the atonement here.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अग्निर्वै᳘ देवा᳘नां व्रत᳘पतिस्त᳘मे᳘वैतदु᳘पधावति त᳘थास्य त᳘न्नाव्रत्यं᳘ कृतं भ᳘वति त᳘था व्रतं न प्र᳘मिनात्ये᳘षोऽ᳘त्र प्रा᳘यश्चित्तिः ॥२४॥
</details>

## २५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓द्यस्मै दीक्षिता᳓याह᳓रेयुर्वा᳓सो वा गां᳓ वा त᳓दभि᳓मन्त्रयेत रा᳓स्वे᳓यत्सोमा᳓ भू᳓यो भरे᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

And if they (people) should bring to this person who has taken up the vow either garment or cow, then he should consecrate it with the mantra, "Oh Soma! Bestow this much. Bring More".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘द्यस्मै दीक्षिता᳘याह᳘रेयुर्वा᳘सो वा गां᳘ वा त᳘दभि᳘मन्त्रयेत रास्वे᳘यत्सोमा भू᳘यो भरे᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

सविता᳓ वै᳓ देवा᳓नां प्रसविता᳓ त᳓थो हास्मा एत᳓त्सवितृ᳓प्रसूतमेव᳓ दा᳓नाय भवति ॥

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

(He chants), "The divine Savitr, the giver of wealth, has bestowed wealth on us". Indeed Savitr is the impeller of the gods. That way, this (gift), verily impelled by Savitr to this (sacrificer) conduces to (further) gift.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

## २६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तं᳓ पुरा᳓स्तमेतोराह दी᳓क्षित वा᳓चं यच्छे᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He (Adhvaryu) says before sunset, "Oh consecrated person (who have taken up the vow)! Restrain your speech". Before sun-rise he says "Oh consecrated person! Restrain your speech".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तं᳘ पुरा᳘स्तमेतोराह दी᳘क्षित वा᳘चं यच्छे᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/24 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

पुरो᳓देतोराह दी᳓क्षित वा᳓चं यच्छे᳓ति सं᳓ततय एवा᳓ह्नैवै᳙त᳓द्रा᳓त्रिं संतनो᳓ति रा᳓त्र्यो [[पु᳓नरहस्तं|पु᳓नरहस्तं᳓]] ना᳓न्य᳓त्र च᳓रन्तमभ्य᳓स्तमियात् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Verily for the purpose of continuity. Thus he continues the night with the day and again the day with night. May not the Sun set on him (the sacrificer) who is moving elsewhere.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

पुरो᳘देतोराह दी᳘क्षित वा᳘चं यच्छे᳘ति सं᳘ततय एवा᳘ह्नै᳘वैतद्रा᳘त्रिꣳ संतनो᳘ति रा᳘त्र्यो पु᳘नर᳘हस्तं᳘ नान्य᳘त्र च᳘रन्तमभ्य᳘स्तमियात्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/27 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[रात्रेर्हैनं|रा᳓त्रेर्हैनं]] त᳓दन्त᳓रियान्नो᳙ एव᳓ [[स्व᳓पन्तमभ्यु᳓दियादह्न|स्व᳓पन्तमभ्यु᳓दियाद᳓ह्न]] उ हैनं त᳓दन्त᳓रियात्त᳓त्प्रतिगु᳓प्यमेवा᳓त्र ना᳓त्र प्रा᳓यश्चित्तिरिवास्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

For thereby he (the Sun) would be cutting him (the sacrificer) off from the night. Certainly let not he (the Sun) rise on him who is sleeping. Thereby verily he would be cutting him off from the day-time.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

रात्रेर्हैनं त᳘दन्त᳘रिया᳘न्नो एव स्व᳘पन्तमभ्यु᳘दियादह्न उ हैनं त᳘दन्त᳘रियात्त᳘त्प्रतिगु᳘प्यमेवा᳘त्र ना᳘त्र प्रा᳘यश्चित्तिरिवास्ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/13 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ न᳓ [[पुरावभृथादपो᳓|पुरा᳓वभृथा᳓दपो᳓]] वाभ्य᳓वेयादभि᳓ चैनं वर्षेत् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Therefore here, it (going out and sleep) must be guarded against. There is indeed no atonement in this matter. Prior to the concluding purificatory bath he should not enter (go towards) water, nor should it rain on him who loves himself.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स न᳘ पुरावमृथा᳘दपो᳘ वाभ्य᳘वेयादभि᳘ चैनं वर्षेत्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स्वकामि᳓नं न᳓ हि᳓ त᳓दवक᳓ल्पते य᳓त्पुरा᳙वभृथा᳓दपो᳓ वाभ्यवेया᳓दभि᳓ चैनं व᳓र्षेत्स्वकामि᳓नं स᳓ परिह्वा᳓लं वा᳓च व᳓दति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

It is not appropriate that before the final purificatory bath (āvabhṛtha) he should enter water or that it may rain on him who has his own desire.

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/11 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

न᳓ प्र᳓सृतां मानुषीं᳓ स᳓ य᳓त्परिह्वा᳓लं वा᳓चं व᳓दति न᳓ प्र᳓सृतां [[मानुषीम्|मानुषी᳓म्]] ॥

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He speaks refined speech and not the widespread human (speech), wherefore he speaks refined speech (of the gods) and not the widespread human (speech).
</details>

## २७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/25 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

देवा᳓ [[वै|वै᳓]] यज्ञे᳓नेमां᳓ जि᳓तिं [[जित्वा|जित्वा᳓]] ये᳓य᳓मेषां᳓ जि᳓तिस्ते᳓ होचु᳓र्ह᳓न्तेदं᳓ त᳓था कर᳓वामहा इ᳓ति य᳓था न इद᳓मनभ्यारुह्यं᳓ मनुष्यै᳙र᳓सदि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Having made this conquest, which conquest is theirs, by means of the sacrifice, they, the gods, said, “Ah! Let us make this (sacrifice) of ours now in such a manner that it may be inaccessible to men”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

देवा वै यज्ञे᳘नेमां जि᳘तिं जित्वा᳘ येय᳘मेषां जि᳘तिस्ते᳘ होचुर्ह᳘न्तेदं त᳘था कर᳘वामहा इ᳘ति य᳘था न इद᳘मनभ्यारुह्यं᳘ मनु᳘ष्यैर᳘सदि᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/23 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

ते᳓ यज्ञ᳓स्य र᳓सं नि᳓र्धी᳓य य᳓था मधुकृ᳓तो [[निर्धयेयुरेवं᳓|निर्ध᳓येयुरेवं᳓]] विदु᳓ह्य यज्ञं᳓ यू᳓पेन योषयित्वा᳓ तिरो᳓ बभूवु᳓स्त᳓द्य᳓देनेना᳓योषयंस्त᳓स्माद्यू᳓पो ना᳓म ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Having completely sucked the essence of the sacrifice, as bees would completely suck (honey) and having emptied it thus, having concealed (obliterated) it with the sacrificial post, they disappeared. Therefore it is well-known as ‘Yūpa’.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ते᳘ यज्ञ᳘स्य र᳘सं निर्धी᳘य य᳘था मधुकृ᳘तो निर्धयेयुरेवं᳘ विदु᳘ह्य यज्ञं यू᳘पेन योषयित्वा᳘ तिरो᳘ बभूवुस्तद्य᳘देनेना᳘योषयꣳस्त᳘स्माद्यू᳘पो ना᳘म ॥२७॥
</details>

## २८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓द्वृ᳓षीणाम᳓नुश्रुतमास᳓ तं᳓ य᳓था यथ᳓र्षयो यज्ञं᳓ सम᳓भरंस्त᳓थायं᳓ यज्ञः᳓ सं᳓भृतो य᳓थो वै᳓ त᳓दृ᳓षयो यज्ञं᳓ सम᳓भरन् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

That was heard by the Ṛṣis. Just as (in different ways) the Ṛṣis collected that sacrifice, in the same manner was this sacrifice collected.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

## २९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ वै᳓ वा᳓गेव᳓ यज्ञः᳓ स᳓ य᳓देव᳓ त᳓त्र यज्ञ᳓स्य देवै᳓र्नि᳓र्धीतं य᳓द्वि᳓दुग्धं त᳓देवै᳙ते᳓न हवि᳓षैते᳓न र᳓सेना᳓प्याययति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Verily speech itself is the sacrifice. What was then (formerly) sucked completely and what was emptied of the sacrifice by the gods, that very thing he (the sacrificer) now replenishes by means of this sacrificial food and by means of this juice.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स वै वा᳘गेव᳘ यज्ञः स य᳘देव त᳘त्र यज्ञ᳘स्य देवैर्नि᳘र्धीतं यद्वि᳘दुग्धं त᳘दे᳘वैते᳘न हवि᳘षैते᳘न र᳘सेना᳘प्याययति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/16 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

न᳓ हा᳓प्याययेद्य᳓त्प्र᳓सृतां मानुषीं᳓ वा᳓चं व᳓देत्त᳓स्मात्परिह्वा᳓लं वा᳓चं [[व᳓दति|वदति]] न᳓ प्र᳓सृतां मानुषी᳓म् ॥

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He would not be replenishing it (filling it up) if he should utter the wide- spread human speech.
</details>

## ३०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/9 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓द्दीक्षितो᳓ ना᳓म वाचे᳓ वा᳓ एष᳓ [[एतद्धीक्षते|एत᳓द्धीक्षते]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Now, (the reason) why he is known as ‘Dīkṣita’ (the one who has undertaken the vow) is that this (sacrifice) now anoints himself thus for the sake of speech. He verily anoints himself for the sacrifice.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘द्दीक्षितो ना᳘म वाचे वा᳘ एष᳘ एतद्धीक्षते
</details>

- य᳓त् (*MW*, *TS*-1461/3383, *RV*-1325/4011, *TB*-821/1735, *TK*-52/227, *AV*-726/3120, *SB-M*-4454/8327, *VS-M*-158/639, *VS-K*-37/456, *KS*-257/818, *MS*-1830/3763)

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

यज्ञा᳓य हि᳓ धी᳓क्षते यज्ञो᳓ हि᳓ वा᳓क्त᳓स्माद्धीक्षितो᳓ ना᳓म धीक्षितो᳓ ह वै᳓ ना᳓मैत᳓द्य᳓द्दीक्षित᳓ इ᳓त्याहुः᳓ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The sacrifice is indeed speech. Therefore he is known as ‘Dīkṣita’ (the anointed). It is verily this (Dīkṣita) that they say is ‘Dīkṣita’ (one who has undertaken the vow). (Second Brāhmaṇa Ends)
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

यज्ञा᳘य हि धी᳘क्षते यज्ञो हि वाक्त᳘स्माद्धीक्षितो ना᳘म धीक्षितो᳘ ह वै ना᳘मैतद्य᳘द्दीक्षित इ᳘त्याहुः ॥३०॥
</details>
